## Jul 29 2026 — Flex-IQ MVP scope lock: the Mutha Goose connection reverts to listen-only

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-07-29-iq-pilot-mvp-listen-only-reversal.md|Jul-29 9:30 AM ET call]] (~31m, Russian; [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]], [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Dzmitry Chudzinau]] (Dima), [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]], [[wiki/people/sergey-tsymanov.md|Sergey Tsimanov]], [[wiki/people/ivan.md|Ivan Shpak]]) Pavel pulled the team together to lock what goes into the nearest **Flex-IQ MVP** (Ivan to scope re-testing, Sergey as the main implementer). This continues the pilot thread from the [[#jul-28-2026--iq-pilot-release-priorities-remote-update-becomes-priority-1-staged-delivery-firmware-hosting-still-open|Jul-28 release-priorities call]]. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]]

- **The Mutha Goose connection reverts to "listen-only" — this reverses the earlier "in-break" decision.** Dima raised edge cases about the MG printer being offline (disconnected, out of paper) while IQ sits "in break" between the MG board and the MG printer. Val cut it off: IQ must have **zero influence** on the Mutha Goose install — "if their printer doesn't work, their printer doesn't work," JVL diagnoses nothing and changes nothing. New scheme: the **MG printer connects directly to the MG system** as it did before JVL was involved; IQ does **not** sit in the middle, does **not** print MG tickets, does **not** print its own receipts, and does **not** touch their system. IQ **only listens** on the side — if it receives a validation signal it shows the ticket on the IQ screen and offers a button to issue a card. Val framed this as a deliberate strategic choice so no one can point fingers at JVL if MG's own equipment fails. This supersedes the [[#jul-28-2026--iq-pilot-release-priorities-remote-update-becomes-priority-1-staged-delivery-firmware-hosting-still-open|Jul-28]] in-break/ticket-pass-through arrangement. Pavel noted the reversal had been decided earlier but not actually implemented yet. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]]
- **Services layer on later, once JVL owns the hardware.** Val's sequencing: only when JVL installs its own bill acceptors (SMBs) and adds a **recorder device**, tying its SMBs to IQ and the recorder, can it layer on validation and services and offer real capabilities (extra accounts, service data, connectivity data). For now JVL makes **no intrusion** — it just runs alongside as an example the operator can use or ignore. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]]
- **Cable consequence.** The current cables were built for the in-break connection and won't work for listen-only; **two cables** are needed and can be re-soldered / re-pinned or given an adapter. Andrei confirmed cables are easy to source or rebuild quickly in Minsk. This becomes a hardware question for [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi Peari]] plus a software change to drop IQ's own ticket printing (keeping only the native Mutha Goose tickets). [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]]
- **Restructure the Flex-IQ work under the capability model.** Pavel had put the terminal work under a product-level grouping ("IP 902 FlexIQ") rather than under Flex-IQ capabilities. Val objected hard: the capability model he and Andrei invest heavily in — **personas → use cases → capabilities → requirements → features, laid out in GitHub** — must be used, not sidelined (Val said he spends about half his day getting that structure right, and it is the single serious project where JVL is trying to adopt it). Andrei clarified that what is written in Confluence is a **"change," not a feature**: a change is a body of logic that can be covered by several capabilities, and one capability can spawn several features (not the reverse). Pavel accepted his AI-assisted pass had wrongly mapped items one-to-one as features and agreed to **create a new IP-level Flex-IQ epic and attach Andrei's documented capabilities**, redoing the mapping. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]]
- **Remote software update is the top MVP priority — and the least-defined item.** Pavel flagged the remote software-update / device-fleet-management capability (promoted to priority 1 on [[#jul-28-2026--iq-pilot-release-priorities-remote-update-becomes-priority-1-staged-delivery-firmware-hosting-still-open|Jul-28]]) as the most important and least-defined MVP item, being built from scratch. He studied **Eclipse hawkBit** — a cloud tool that manages a fleet of devices, monitors their versions and statuses, and pushes software updates. Dima and Andrei are evaluating alternatives in parallel; the architecture still needs settling. Val stressed it must be **polished** because there will be many terminals: if updates go smoothly the other rough edges slide by, but if updates are hard, every problem becomes painful and visible. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]]
- **Schedule and scope discipline.** Val was firm: do **not** treat Aug 1 (a Saturday) as a hard deadline, **no Friday release, and no installation at any location before Tuesday (Aug 4)** — "hurry slowly." It is a raw beta release; JVL needs only one harness/sample and the minimal set of capabilities, with an operator who understands it is unfinished. He also told Andrei to **stop sending new capability documents and ideas** to developers during the MVP crunch (every document pulls four pairs of eyes off delivery), deferred the **PIN-per-location / location-change idea** to a separate meeting, and reiterated that he alone directs scope for the JVL project — nothing is to be assigned to anyone without him. **§15.8:** clear — Flex-IQ MVP scope, connection scheme, and update-tooling economics only; no named-person compensation. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Pilot_MVP_Listen_Only_Reversal-July-29-2026.md]]

## Jul 30 2026 — can we verify an IQ unit is physically at its registered location? (parked for the pilot)

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-07-30-iq-unit-location-binding.md|Jul-30 10:01 AM ET "Spec_gaps" call]] (~23m; [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]], [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan Martinez]]) Andrei raised a **design gap**: the system cannot verify that a given IQ unit is **physically at the location it is registered to**. Today the unit is bound to a location **manually in the payment hub**, which guarantees nothing — nothing stops an operator moving a unit from a registered location A to an unregistered location B; cards issued at B would still pass and be billed to A. This is the IQ-binding thread from the [[wiki/meetings/2026-07-21-unbind-vs-freeze-and-offline-transactions.md|Jul-21 unbind-vs-freeze]] and [[wiki/meetings/2026-07-21-escrow-model-and-owned-network-vision.md|Jul-21 escrow/owned-network]] discussions. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]]

- **How registration actually works.** JVL sends a location's details (license number, address, contact) to **InComm**; InComm verifies (reply in ~24–72 hours) and returns **secret keys**; those keys plus the location data are set up as a **location profile** in the payment hub. Each card-issue request sends those keys and the location info, and InComm returns a **success pass** if they match. The unit itself has **no awareness of its physical location** beyond that manual link. Andrei corrected Juan that InComm does **not** regulate the device — only that you cannot **issue gift cards** at an unauthorized location; the device itself is up to JVL. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]]
- **Why InComm no longer catches this.** Juan explained InComm used to verify (up to ~36 hours) that a location was **GLC-authorized**, but stopped — the burden of checking thousands of locations now falls on the **master under lottery penalty**. Juan's view: if the location is lawfully authorized and JVL still gets the card event and bills for it, the mismatch may not matter much — the bill goes to the right email even if the wrong address, and the operator isn't avoiding the service fees. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]]
- **Manual vs operator-driven binding — Val prefers operator-driven.** Andrei framed two approaches: keep **manual** binding (JVL must know each unit's destination up front) or push binding onto the **operator** (JVL just ships units and operators distribute them). Val preferred the operator-driven approach, noting it mirrors how operators already move JVL machines and reassign **CoMIDs** without telling JVL. A unit could only work at a location already registered with JVL. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]]
- **The "quiet listen" location read.** Val said an **LTE modem's triangulation** is quite reliable and, combined with the **Mutha Goose serial number**, would give a fairly reliable read on a unit's location — and JVL should "quietly" listen to it rather than actively police it ("even if we care, I don't think we should care… however, the LTE modem does provide this information"). This is Val's proposed answer to the standing open question of how to detect that an IQ changed location (raised Jul-21/22, where GPS-indoors and external-IP were rejected); Pavel considers triangulation unreliable, Val disagrees from experience. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]]
- **The collections angle: registering credit against a company's title.** Juan worried that a master could sell a location, move the unit, and dispute the bill; a legal claim would stand but **enforcing collection** against an operator with no assets is the real problem. Val's idea: when credit is extended to an operator, **register it against the company's title** so the company cannot simply be sold — JVL does not do this for machines today but should. The friction: the operator will demand the title be discharged and may walk, so it is "thin ice." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]]
- **The decommissioning blind spot.** An InComm **decommissioning** step exists, but JVL only learns a unit is decommissioned if it is told, or if a new JVL device registers at the same location; otherwise the unit just shows **zero revenue**. Juan flagged this as a possible theft/abuse vector — someone could run a Flex-IQ at home, encode cards and sell them below value. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]]
- **Decision: park it for the pilot.** Val's direction: don't over-engineer a solution in a vacuum. For the **5-machine pilot** the gap is not important (one location per operator, close communication), and the current imperfect solution lets JVL launch pilots without issue. Get to a real pilot, gather real-world cases, and revisit — the topic was explicitly parked, "written… it will not slip from our radar." Val also floated needing a **minimum monthly charge per unit** (~$10, figure TBD — "$10 or $100") so parked units still generate some revenue, since an operator could buy a unit cheaply and then sit on it. The main pilot blocker is the **production environment** Andrei is still trying to get from InComm; he is pushing them daily and won't release until it is tested on production, and the **rewired cable** from the Jul-29 listen-only solution is ready for him to test right away. **§15.8:** clear — device/location-binding, InComm process, and per-unit billing economics only; no named-person compensation. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]]

## Jul 31 2026 — how each transactional product is demoed at the Georgia expo

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-07-31-georgia-expo-product-demo-plan.md|Jul-31 12:00 PM ET expo-planning call]] ([[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]], [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan]], [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]]) the team defined the demo flow for each of the four transactional products it will show at the Aug-18 Georgia expo. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Georgia_Expo_Product_Demo_Plan-July-31-2026.md]]

- **Flex IQ — the connected workflow.** Present IQ in its **connected version**: machines wired together through Flexports with one **Mother Goose**, issuing cards using **test cards that do not look like real ones** — the point is to show the workflow, not real card stock. **Two games** are enough to show it is more than one-to-one. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Georgia_Expo_Product_Demo_Plan-July-31-2026.md]]
- **Payment-hub dashboard — show the "future" extended version.** Andrei walked his dashboard mockup: an operational interface an operator opens each morning — location setup, IQ online/offline status + software version, a money-flow overview by period, transaction detail, top locations by performance and share, top gaming machines, IQ fleet state (units online vs offline), and a location map for planning site visits. Val was initially hesitant that leaning on reporting could look "skimpy," but after seeing the mockup agreed it warrants a proper screen (a **monitor next to the laptop in the IQ area**). Juan rated it a marketing advantage over a **PAX device**. Decisions: show **only the extended "future" version on Mother Goose** (no second version), and **do not display a live or fake transaction in the reports** (the booth is not an authorized location). [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Georgia_Expo_Product_Demo_Plan-July-31-2026.md]]
- **SMIB dashboard offered but deferred.** Andrei offered to build a **SMIB (non-Mother-Goose)** dashboard mockup that could also show **money-in**, not just money-out. Juan's ruling: **sell IQ first**, offer SMIB as a later upgrade — one step at a time. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Georgia_Expo_Product_Demo_Plan-July-31-2026.md]]
- **Recycler demo — QR-ticket flow with a faked Skill-Pay conversion.** The show flow: insert **$20**, print a **QR-code ticket**, play across machines with Flexport (traverse — Georgia House Bill 353 calls this "replay"), create a win, then for the demo issue a **Skill Pay** card via IQ, present it to the recycler, and the recycler dispenses cash. The proper September use case issues the Skill Pay card **automatically from the recycler** instead of IQ. The Skill Pay card (coming from **InComm** around September) is ATM-ready and convertible to cash; **the recycler acts as an ATM** (JVL becomes an ATM-network signatory). For the show they will **fake the conversion** to Skill Pay (subject to InComm's permission) using a hand-painted/laminated distinct (e.g. green) card. Because the recycler has **no card dispenser** today, **IQ would need QR-reading added** for the demo; Val said the recycler's mechanical design must evolve to house an **optional QR printer plus a card dispenser**. On fees, Val wants to stay **silent at the show** and set them once the market is understood. There is also an open **legislative question** of whether money can be taken from the card to credit machine play directly, versus needing a printed QR ticket as the carrying medium. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Georgia_Expo_Product_Demo_Plan-July-31-2026.md]]
- **Dispenser — a springboard into other markets.** Shown for **Texas/Nebraska**, not Georgia: start a session on the dispenser, get an e-wallet + QR ticket, traverse machines, return to the dispenser, scan, get cash. The recycler will **not** read fake bills (real bill acceptor); the dispenser might, so Andrei will ask **Roberto** how they demo it. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Georgia_Expo_Product_Demo_Plan-July-31-2026.md]]
- **Flex Pay — no concerns.** Already shown at multiple shows; it dispenses cards and works with Flexport. Andrei will set up recycler, dispenser and Flex Pay in the Georgia office and work with them daily, starting dispenser testing ASAP. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Georgia_Expo_Product_Demo_Plan-July-31-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — product-demo / device-flow economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Jul 31 2026 — Flex-Recycler show readiness: an intermediate card screen and the CPI/JCM denomination constraint

Immediately after the expo call, Val took the recycler-readiness question to engineering on the [[wiki/meetings/2026-07-31-flex-recycler-georgia-show-readiness.md|Jul-31 1:30 PM ET call]] ([[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]], [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi Peari]]; in Russian): can the Flex-Recycler be ready to demo at the Aug-18 show? [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_Recycler_Georgia_Show_Readiness-July-31-2026.md]]

- **Pavel's readiness read.** Both the IQ-issued-card flow and the ideal recycler-issued-card flow are technically possible but **not realistic on the timeline**. Pavel proposed a **third scenario**: before dispensing cash, show an extra **screen indicating a digital card is being issued** (not yet on the market) with a button to convert it to cash. It is not fully "honest" (properly it should be card first, then cash) but it is the only way to actually pay out what is on the e-wallet. The QR-ticket-to-cash flow already works in some form today; the architecture with Flex IQ and the recycler as **separate units** would need new code on **both sides** to link them. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_Recycler_Georgia_Show_Readiness-July-31-2026.md]]
- **Hardware state.** One recycler is built in Georgia and a second is ordered there; metal and parts are on hand, with mandates to start assembling **slim cabinets** — they want **two** (one for the show, one "for the bank"). Only **one dispenser** is on hand, so a second must be ordered. Nuance: the Flex dispenser **has not been powered up/checked in a while**, and Genadi noted **no software is installed** on it — it must be verified. The recycler must sit in the **slim cabinet** (better made slim; can sit in front of a site). [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_Recycler_Georgia_Show_Readiness-July-31-2026.md]]
- **The CPI/JCM denomination-configuration problem.** Val wants the recycler set to **"$5 and $20."** Genadi reported **CPI's logic differs**: the **first drum must be fully filled with the minimum denomination** (so a drum could be all $1, or a second drum split with up to three denominations) — not a clean four-denomination setup. Pavel said this is **not 100% settled** — he spoke with **Sasha**, who is studying whether any denomination can go in the first drum and has not ruled it out; Genadi said CPI's senior confirmed the minimum-first rule last week. Capacity estimate: one drum of $5s ≈ **$300**; splitting the second drum gives roughly **$600 + $300 ≈ $1,200**. Val called the minimum-first rule bad design "no customer can use" and wants **cassette 1 = $20, cassette 2 = $5/$10** — Genadi to open the conversation with **CPI/JCM** on Monday and ask them to change their code. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_Recycler_Georgia_Show_Readiness-July-31-2026.md]]
- **Direct cash dispensing deferred.** Genadi asked whether cash can be dispensed from the recycler **directly** (not only via a card). Val: for now **only via card** — direct cash dispensing requires financing the dispenser and new legislation, not now. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_Recycler_Georgia_Show_Readiness-July-31-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — recycler-readiness / cabinet / denomination-config economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Jul 31 2026 — network sponsorship for the ATM-style dispense: Herring Bank and InComm's Prize Play card

The same day, Val had an introductory call with **Herring Bank** ([[wiki/meetings/2026-07-31-herring-bank-prize-play-card.md|Jul-31 Prize Play card call]], with bank principal **Campbell** and liaison **Maya Fuentes**) about the network-sponsorship layer under JVL's ATM-style cash-dispensing machines — the piece that makes the **recycler-as-ATM** and card-dispense flows legal. The full banking relationship is on [[wiki/organizations/herring-bank.md]] (Jul-31); the product-side takeaways: [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Herring_Bank_Prize_Play_Card-July-31-2026.md]]

- **It's the card activity that needs sponsorship, not the machine.** When a **network card initiates a cash dispense**, that card transaction is what requires a sponsoring bank: the card triggers it, **Herring authorizes/clears it**, the machine dispenses, and settlement follows. Herring is a **principal network member bank** that can hold balances, transmit money and dispense/receive cash, and integrates to JVL's own software via API. This answers the recurring "does the recycler/dispenser need ATM-network registration?" question raised across the [[wiki/meetings/2026-07-09-bill-recycler-atm-registration.md|Jul-9 bill-recycler ATM-registration call]] and the expo-demo Skill-Pay flow above. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Herring_Bank_Prize_Play_Card-July-31-2026.md]]
- **The Prize Play card = the ATM-ready card in the recycler flow.** InComm's **Prize Play card** (the same "Skill Pay" card the [[#jul-31-2026--how-each-transactional-product-is-demoed-at-the-georgia-expo|expo-demo section above]] describes as ATM-ready and cashable at the recycler, coming from InComm ~September) is the product driving this. Use cases: a player **cashing out from the card at a machine**, and **machines accepting the card directly**. Val has **not decided** whether machines should accept all network cards or only loaded cards — he will follow market requirements. Herring wants **at least one JVL machine in its lab** to test/certify. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Herring_Bank_Prize_Play_Card-July-31-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — network-sponsorship / card-product / device-integration economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Aug 12 2026 — Fiery Pay competitor analysis (Afshad/Aristocrat) and JVL's recycler answer

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-12-fiery-pay-competitive-analysis.md|Aug-12 3:31pm ET call]] (~31 min; [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan Martinez]], [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe Miranda Giorgi]]; [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]] invited but absent) the team reviewed a competitor payment system, **Fiery Pay** (run by "**Afshad**," linked with **Aristocrat**), turning up in Georgia COAM locations, and how JVL should answer it. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Fiery_Pay_Competitive_Analysis-August-12-2026.md]]

- **How Fiery Pay is understood to work.** No printer; it ties into the machine's **bill acceptor** (via a protocol like **JCM or MEI**), because the bill acceptor is what issues credits. Felipe's observed flow at a location ~2 hours away (near Macon): cash into the bill acceptor → play → on cash-out the credits go onto a **loyalty/e-wallet card** sitting on the machine → the player goes to a **kiosk** that converts player points to a **gift card** (a **$2 fee**) or a gift card back to player points. The gift card is a standard prize-pay / MasterCard-type card spendable anywhere (e.g. Walmart), but the player can also bring it back, scan the barcode, convert to player points, and load credits onto a machine; partial amounts split between gift card and machine. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Fiery_Pay_Competitive_Analysis-August-12-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Fiery_Pay_Competitive_Analysis-August-12-2026.md]]
- **Why the team sees it as weak and risky.** Val called the send-credits-back-to-a-machine step a **"legal-ish" gymnastics workaround** — operators treat it as a "transfer" point rather than a redemption point. He and Juan flagged heavy **fraud exposure** (money can be sent back to an unattended machine someone else then sits at; no real systems check on amounts), and Val's larger point: after all the gymnastics the player still walks away with a **cash-spendable gift card**, defeating the purpose of keeping money in play. Juan noted the space is essentially **unregulated** — GLC has said it does not control what these kiosks do. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Fiery_Pay_Competitive_Analysis-August-12-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Fiery_Pay_Competitive_Analysis-August-12-2026.md]]
- **Open legal question.** Nobody could explain how a **non-cash MasterCard gift card is legally converted into player points/cash** — it appears to need a merchant ID / reseller status and looks like a "fake transaction" run to empty the card; unclear whether Fiery Pay or the location holds the merchant ID. **Fiery Payments** is a broader payments company (ATM + card processing across Visa, MasterCard, Amex, Discover, Union Pay, JCB), not just a COAM product. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Fiery_Pay_Competitive_Analysis-August-12-2026.md]]
- **Market read on competitor redemption systems.** Felipe hears appetite for this kind of system from several operators; the "Tito" hype is cooling. Solutions now talked about: **Batson (Light & Wonder)** — *(name-collision flag: this "Batson" is a Light & Wonder product, NOT [[wiki/people/arkady-batson.md|Arkady Batson]] the JVL person — do not conflate)*; **Easylinks** (ticket printed in the back); **Fiery Pay** (Afshad / Aristocrat link — the most popular); and **Access** (with printer). One location Felipe recently serviced is pulling out its printers and kiosk to install a competitor system; Felipe saw the box labelled "**JVL kit for flex**," which he found odd. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Fiery_Pay_Competitive_Analysis-August-12-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Fiery_Pay_Competitive_Analysis-August-12-2026.md]]
- **JVL's answer — recycler + flex port around the "replay" wallet.** JVL's **"replay"** feature is its version of the temporary wallet. To print the initial QR code the location needs a **recycler** (not necessarily a FlexPay kiosk). Val's stated winning combination is a **recycler + a flex port** (which also enables sophisticated operator reporting); **IQ is optional** ("you probably don't need it," though Val expects operators will still take it). The recycler solution depends on InComm's price-play card. Val relayed a message received during the call from **John Hasel at Income** (= [[wiki/organizations/incom.md|InComm]]'s Jason Hall, per the same-day [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-12-incomm-skill-pay-recycler-strategy.md|InComm call]]): the current card is **Prize Play / Prize Pay**; the new one is **Skill Pay**, available from **mid-September**, which would let JVL implement the full solution around **November**. Val never spoke directly with Afshad (Afshad refused to sign the NDA); the back-and-forth was on Felipe's side. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Fiery_Pay_Competitive_Analysis-August-12-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Fiery_Pay_Competitive_Analysis-August-12-2026.md]]

**Decision (direction, no formal sign-off):** JVL's competitive package for these locations is a **recycler + a flex port**, built around the "replay" temporary wallet and InComm's price-play/Skill Pay card; **IQ optional**, not required. **§15.8:** clear — competitor/market/device economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Aug 14 2026 — first full Flex-Recycler cash cycle works; the MEI recycler is scrapped

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-14-flex-recycler-cycle-test-mei-rework.md|Aug-14 12:00 PM ET alignment call]] (~9 min; [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]], [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan]], [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]], [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]], [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina]]) Andrei reported the first full recycler cash cycle, and Val decided to drop the current MEI recycler. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_Recycler_Cycle_Test_MEI_Rework-August-14-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Recycler_Cycle_Test_MEI_Rework-August-14-2026.md]]

- **The cycle worked end to end.** Yesterday the team completed a full assembly and connected the recycler to **two gaming machines fitted with the new flex ports**. Use case: a player put **$5** into the cash dispenser, created and confirmed a PIN, and printed a **TITO-style banknote ticket (not a QR code)**; at a flex-port machine they scanned the ticket, confirmed the PIN, transferred $5 (5 credits), played, won **$15**, and cashed the $15 back to the e-wallet; back at the recycler they cashed out real money. Because the bank held only one $5 note, the machine dispensed at most $5 and showed a $10 balance still playable. Val called it "excellent news." [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_Recycler_Cycle_Test_MEI_Rework-August-14-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Recycler_Cycle_Test_MEI_Rework-August-14-2026.md]]
- **What Val wants tested next.** The first run used only one machine; Val wants the test re-run across **both machines, traversing back and forth a couple of times**, to prove the e-wallet communicates as a player moves between machines, then cash out the full balance (kept under $50) showing mixed bills — an ideal **$55 payout of two $20s and three $5s** (this setup has no $10s). First Andrei must finish the assembly: boot the dispenser (the latest build from **Alexander** is uploaded — which Alexander unspecified; likely [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Alexander Biratko]] the recycler build lead, not owner-confirmed, flagged), confirm it sees all parameters/parts, then finalize the recycler while Minsk is online. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_Recycler_Cycle_Test_MEI_Rework-August-14-2026.md]]
- **Decision — scrap the MEI recycler, build the right one.** Val ruled the **MEI recycler's mechanics and construction are wrong** and JVL will not use it; there is no time to get the correct unit before the show, but the proper recycler — one that **supports multiple cassettes** — will be built instead. MEI will not supply the product; JVL already has it. The unit is being **sent to Minsk to be redone**, targeting a **first beta sample around mid-September**, timed to coincide with **InComm's Skill Pay card** introduction (continuing the Aug-12 recycler answer above). [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_Recycler_Cycle_Test_MEI_Rework-August-14-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Recycler_Cycle_Test_MEI_Rework-August-14-2026.md]]

## Aug 21 2026 — the GOAM show read on Flex IQ: wrapped cards break the reader, and everyone asks for replay and split

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-21-post-goam-show-recap.md|Aug-21 post-GOAM recap]] (recording 1, 4:01–5:45 PM ET; [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]], [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan]], [[wiki/people/felipe-miranda.md|Felipe]]) Andrei reported back from the Georgia show. The ruling that came out of it is [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-21-release-flex-iq-as-is-place-units-collect-facts.md]].

- **Market reaction to the device itself was strong.** Operators "really like the design," reach to touch and tap it, and when they lift it "they feel that it is kind of heavy, well-built, and they admit it." They immediately understand the value of a transaction that needs no manual input — "it avoids fraud and mistakes." But asked directly whether they see fraud or mistyping with PAX today, "They do not admit it. They say, no, it works well. We don't see any issues." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **The wrapped-card blocker.** InComm's product manager for the cards told Andrei that cards shipped **for a kiosk** (locked in a protected box, unreachable by cashier or player) go out **unwrapped as a plain stack**, but cards kept at a cashier desk or otherwise publicly accessible may **only** ship **wrapped** — so the number is not visible. IQ's magnetic-stripe/swipe verification therefore cannot activate them: "right now IQ cannot work with unwrapped cards and it will never be allowed by income." Andrei's two options: add a **dispenser** to IQ, or make IQ **read barcodes** (the wrap carries a visible barcode). Juan: "I think it should support both, anyway." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
  - Physically, the existing optical reader sits underneath and cannot be presented with the wrap's barcode; Val walked Andrei through rotating and inserting the pack diagonally on camera, could not confirm a read (the unit was switched off), and concluded "It's okay, I know how to do it… we can install a second barcode reader. Not a big deal." A **USB 2D barcode scanner** (Juan) or a hand "gun scanner" (Felipe) is the interim fallback. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
  - Andrei found an upside in wrapped cards: per InComm, activating a wrapped card "equals automatically to the point that player has already accepted the… rules," so **no player-facing screen is needed** — a cashier-facing screen suffices. Juan still wanted the amount loaded shown to the player; Andrei: "it shouldn't be touch. It might be something simpler." Felipe noted PAX prints a receipt showing what was loaded (he had collected from a PAX once and got one). Andrei's conclusion: "if we will be able to print out tickets, we don't need a player-facing screen." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **The replay / split demand.** "every single person we talked to, every single operator master, when we were discussing IQs, they said, does it do replay?" — meaning moving a player from one machine to another. Separately "many of them are asking… can you split the transaction?" so a player gets part on the card and the rest as cash or lottery tickets; IQ does neither today. Felipe added a third ask: **multiple gift-card brands** ("what if I won an Amazon gift card or a Walmart gift card"), which Andrei put on the back burner as a different question about integrating other processors. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
  - PAX supports split only because entry is manual: "if the voucher from Motor Goose says $10, the cashier may input 5 on the card, verify the card to give it to player and give $5 as lottery tickets." Andrei argued IQ could split **without** manual entry and stay auditable. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
  - Juan's read on why locations want split: it lets them wash their hands — "if it's $100 cash out and we put 50 on the card, we're only going to reconcile the $50. In our world, that's all that happened. The other 50, that's on them." Val: "GLC can absolutely audit… These are your payouts. This amount should be on card. Everything else is [expletive]"; Juan: "but they don't want to audit." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **Competitor flows named.** **EasyLynx** (transcribed "Easylink"/"easy links") does replay "through a Tito ticket printed in the back end"; **Fiery Pay** does it "through sending credits to a machine number one or two or three," which Juan called "so dangerous" and Val "the wrong thing." Juan added that Felipe had heard "a lot of people are buying off on easy links and it's picking up traction." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **The QR / e-wallet direction — agreed in principle, flow not locked.** All four converged on ports in every machine plus a QR-code or barcode wallet the player carries. Val: "that something is installing [ports] into every machine"; Juan: "And the QR code, it lets you move from machine to machine." Val called a QR/barcode temporary wallet "a hot idea. I'm all about it." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
  - **Val's preferred flow:** keep the bill acceptor on the machine, play, and issue the e-wallet on cash-out — "approach the machine, have a bill acceptor, put money inside the bill acceptor, start a game, and on the cash out, get an e-wallet. That would be my desirable solution." Carrier medium: a printed ticket, or NFC / a QR shown on the player's phone — but "that requires a presentation of a personal communication device, which most players hesitate" for fear of giving up personal information. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
  - **Andrei's variant:** a simple standalone printer in the gaming area (a "black box" with one button) issuing a **zero-value** QR ticket the player then tops up at a bill validator. Val rejected it: "It's not going to work… you initiate first purchase from a recycler or a cashier. First, you need a wallet." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
  - **Felipe's variant, on relationship grounds:** issue the wallet at the counter on cash-out, because "for a lot of masters, that interaction with players is very, very important. And that's why they don't like kiosks," with "90% of locations… convenience stores or gas stations" needing to upsell. Juan agreed: "the kiosk disconnects the player from the cashier and the ability to upsell them on anything else." Felipe also cited the rule that gaming "cannot be more than 50%… of their sales." No flow was locked. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
  - Constraints raised against printers on machines: Juan — "There's not a printer located in every game in Georgia" and "30% of those machines in Georgia do not even support a printer from a technical standpoint." Juan also observed that Andrei's black-box printer "is the FlexPay kiosk without the card dispenser. We already have that," and Andrei's counter was cost: "kiosk costs 8, $9,000 and flex IQ cost 9 times less" — plus the space saving (Felipe). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
  - **Port vs SMIB, restated.** Andrei asked whether SMIB could be integrated into the port; Val: "you don't need to, because the port is elaborate version of SMIB… the port is the SMIP plus the optical reader plus the display." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **Mutha Goose is losing ground.** Asked whether Georgia locations exist without Mutha Goose, Felipe: yes — "those that have… kiosk and and using dual SAS, they don't have model groups." Val: "And Mother Goose is losing locations… eventually, we'll be out of business unless they… Do something different." Juan: "If you've got modern machines in a kiosk, you don't need Mother Goose." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **Recycler feedback — interest is high, security perception is not.** Val refused to detail the recycler ("the product is not ready"), but Andrei reported the show unit was "the slimmest device in the whole export," that on day two visitors ran the whole e-wallet flow unaided, and that Felipe's "board of directors of Gamora" (name as transcribed; the company is not otherwise identified) "were like… there were dollar signs in their eyes just seeing this device and how flawless it looks like." What they want changed: **thicker metal** — Val: "they would like to see thicker metal." Visitors also said to **remove the PIN code**: "let's remove the PIN code. It looks like we don't need it. Less friction… quicker money in, quicker money out" — the same conclusion Val reached three days later in [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-24-drop-pin-from-evoucher-flow.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **Ports drew good questions.** Asked which machines ports work with, "when they hear that it works with all the game machines currently in the market, it sounds really promising to them"; some asked whether JVL installs them or they do. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **A Texas test site is secured for the transactional products** — 20 machines taking Flex IQ, Flex ports and the Flex Dispenser, first week of October. Val parked the detail: "That's not for this conversation. That's the next thing." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]] Felipe reported little Texas interest at the Georgia show and suggested discussing a booth for **AMOA**. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **Card ordering is the location's job.** Juan confirmed with Andrei: JVL orders the **first** pack when registering a location ("when we install, we will also have the first pack of cars"), shipped to Andrei's office and delivered with the hardware; "after that, it is their responsibility to refill it." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Georgia_Recap_and_JVL_Dollars-August-21-2026.md]]
- **§15.8:** clear — device, market and gift-card economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Aug 24 2026 — Flex-Port-Mini: a 2nd-SAS-only port, the QR eVoucher flow, and the local-network problem

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-24-flex-port-mini-capabilities.md|Aug-24 Flex-Port-Mini session]] (Russian; [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi Peari]], [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]]) the team designed a new, cheap member of the Flex port family. Rulings: [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-24-flex-port-mini-via-second-sas-only.md]] (product), [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-24-free-hardware-fixed-monthly-fee-model.md]] (commercial model), [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-24-drop-pin-from-evoucher-flow.md]] (PIN).

- **What the Mini is.** Same footprint as today's port ("фудпринт такой же как… Сегодня"), no full screen, connected **only** to the machine's second SAS port, so the heavy bill-acceptor in-line wiring disappears. Genadi's summary of why that matters: "нету все этой сложной подключ вырывания. Этого билактора это же мейзинг" — no complicated cutting into the bill acceptor, which is amazing. Val: "Это это то же как сми, только он наружу торчит" — it is the same as a SMIB, only it sticks out (it has to, for the scanner). Genadi: "только наружу с маленьким экранчиком." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Port_Mini_Capabilities_Design-August-24-2026.md]]
- **Player use-case (Val's, stated twice and then fixed as the spec).** A player hands the cashier, say, **$20** and receives a **QR code** — printed on a ticket, sent to a phone, or shown on screen to photograph — then presents it at any machine's optical scanner and all the money loads at once; on cash-out all of it comes off at once. Val listed **four** ways the cashier can issue it: print a ticket; scan any reusable plastic card carrying a unique barcode (a purely **local** carrier — Pavel: it cannot carry the server URL); send to a phone number; or display a QR to photograph. Pavel saw only the display-and-photograph route as realistic; Val pushed back that printing is fine because "принтеры у всех логичных сегодня есть." A fourth option they agreed on: the location pre-prints **blank** codes and just scans one. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Port_Mini_Capabilities_Design-August-24-2026.md]]
  - Val's aside on incentives: the player can be pushed toward the phone route with promo credit — "если ты будешь пользоваться своим телефоном и тебе дадим промогрэт." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Port_Mini_Capabilities_Design-August-24-2026.md]]
  - Pavel's objection to a "show me the balance, then cancel" feature: Val ruled it out — "все деньги ты заглатываешь или все деньги ты выдаёшь" — and offered the phone-scan balance check instead. Pavel: "Если хочешь посмотреть статус его лета, иди на терминал и смотри там." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Port_Mini_Capabilities_Design-August-24-2026.md]]
- **Cash still enters through the operator's own bill acceptor** as it does today; the Mini takes the pay-out off the second SAS as an **AFT transaction** to the wallet via "минисасссервер, который будет крутиться на флексайти" — a mini SAS server running on Flex IQ (Pavel). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Port_Mini_Capabilities_Design-August-24-2026.md]]
- **The local network is the main unsolved problem.** The ports need a local network to reach Flex IQ / the server so vouchers can be validated; today that is Ethernet plus a router installed at every location. Val: "Без локальной сети мы не можем вализировать… локальная сеть нужна"; Pavel: "Да вот это точно." Options, and what was decided about them, are on the product decision page. Genadi's observation that Mutha Goose already runs short CAT5 links machine-to-machine in a chain is why Val prefers reusing that wiring. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Port_Mini_Capabilities_Design-August-24-2026.md]]
- **Two-stage rollout.** Stage 1 = Mini + Flex-Port with wired CAT5 to each machine on the existing protocol. Stage 2 (not committed) = drop Flex IQ and let any mobile device with a cashier app issue vouchers, which Pavel warned needs a modem in each SMIB port and puts the cost back. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Port_Mini_Capabilities_Design-August-24-2026.md]]
- **Val's tempo.** He asked whether the board could be laid out "завтра утром"; Pavel said the code would not be ready by then but Val insisted the board layout would be. Val then asked what he had missed, because "я хочу сесть за бульдозер сейчас" — he wants to sit down at the bulldozer now. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Port_Mini_Capabilities_Design-August-24-2026.md]]
- **§15.8:** clear — component, device and market economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Aug 26 2026 — the product scope declared to InComm, the first live location, and a bigger CPI recycler nobody has a library for

Two separate Aug-26 calls touch this page.

**From the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-26-incomm-skill-pay-atm-guidance.md|InComm follow-up]] (3:00 PM ET):**

- **Only two transactional products go to Georgia in the next 12 months: Flex IQ and Flex Port.** Val, asked by Jason Hall to name every product JVL is bringing to the market: "In the foreseeable future, 12 months from today, we're seeing two products, **Flex IQ and Flex Port**" — Flex IQ described as "the **tabletop POS**", Flex Port as mounted **on the outside** of a machine (Val first said "inside of a machine"; [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan]] corrected him and Val accepted: "I meant, I meant in the machine outside dashboard"). Full ruling on [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-certify-only-flex-iq-and-flex-port-with-incomm.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]
- **Every product now carries a UX-certification gate before it can activate InComm cards** — UX screenshots plus a video of the full operation, reviewed by InComm; and for card-dispensing kiosks a terms-and-conditions kit to apply first. Any later interface change repeats the loop with a video, a test receipt and a test card. Detail on [[wiki/organizations/incom.md]] (Aug-26). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]
- **The first real customer location launches on POS with the black Prize Pay card** ("in a matter of a week or so", Andrei Klimovich), because Skill Pay is not yet available in the wrapped POS form. Skill Pay's wrapped cards arrive **late September**; kiosk cards **early September**. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]
- **The recycler/ATM branch of the device catalog is blocked on licensing, and InComm has ruled against pairing it with a COAM kiosk.** Val confirmed JVL is working on an "**ATM Redemption Service**" but that it "cannot be implemented. Without us being licensed as an **ATM operator**. Which requires a bank." The constraint InComm imposed — no cash out of a COAM redemption device, and no coordinated kiosk+ATM pair from the same entity — is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-no-cash-from-coam-redemption-device-for-skill-pay.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Skill_Pay_ATM_Guidance-August-26-2026.md]]

**From the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-26-alignment-planning-nova-september-releases.md|morning alignment call]] (7:40 AM ET, Russian):**

- **A bigger-capacity recycler is being sourced from CPI, and its library will not work.** Val told [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi]] to pick a different recycler from **CPI** — one with a large capacity that "может расти в капасаре" (can grow in capacity) — and believes Genadi has already ordered or is ordering it. [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]], who spoke to Genadi about it the day before, named the model only vaguely: "**это какойто b nr или BNP помоему модель BNR**" — some BNR or BNP, he thinks BNR — and reported Genadi's verdict: "**он уже сказал, что нет, это будет несовместим с нашей библиотекой**" (incompatible with our library), though he was promised the needed code/library. *(Model recorded as spoken — "BNR or BNP, he thinks BNR" — not asserted.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Alignment_Planning_NOVA_and_Sept_Releases-August-26-2026.md]]
- **Pavel's precedent: they will have to reverse-engineer it, as they did on ARM.** "мы это делали для арм платформы. И мы, по сути, в чистом виде библиотеку не использовали. Мы полностью переписали то, что они нам прислали… Полностью сделали реверс инжиниринг" — for the ARM platform they used no vendor library as-shipped, rewrote everything sent to them and fully reverse-engineered it. "нам готового решения не будет" — there will be no ready-made solution. He added that reverse-engineering **with Claude goes quickly and is easy to check**; the real difficulty is that "**удалённо с этим сложно работать**" — it is hard to do remotely, with the physical device elsewhere. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Alignment_Planning_NOVA_and_Sept_Releases-August-26-2026.md]]
- **The remote-work problem, unsolved.** Val asked whether a more reliable **development station** could be built — better internet, some video interaction — and noted the more technical [[wiki/people/sebastian.md|Sebastian]] is arriving in Georgia. Pavel's counter: you can sit a person there and tell them "всунь деньгу… теперь там должно выехать сколькото купюр посчитай их" (insert the money, now some notes should come out, count them) — possible, "но это лишний человек. **Робота у нас нету удалённого**" (that is an extra person; we have no remote robot). Val: "я не вижу пока другого пути" — he sees no other path yet; the device will be shipped to Minsk, and even the fastest shipping is "**гдето 30 45 дней**." Both men parked it. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Alignment_Planning_NOVA_and_Sept_Releases-August-26-2026.md]]
- **§15.8:** clear — device, card-programme and shipping economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Aug 27 2026 — Flex-IQ must be able to switch clearing houses without re-approving the software

On the seven-minute [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-27-flex-iq-multiple-clearinghouse-selection.md|Aug-27 call]] ([[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]]) Val added one capability to Flex-IQ: at device registration, a **selection of which clearing house does the clearance** — not [[wiki/organizations/incom.md|InComm]] by default. Full ruling, including the per-device scope and the explicit not-in-the-MVP and don't-contact-other-clearinghouses-yet limits: [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-27-flex-iq-multiple-clearinghouses-selectable-per-device.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_IQ_Multiple_Clearinghouse_Selection-August-27-2026.md]]

- **The device-architecture consequence** is the one Val stated as the hard requirement: "We just need to have this capability that we could easily switch on a fly. **I don't want to be re-approving the software. I don't want to be designing the software again.**" Scope is **per device, not per location** — "So let's say IQ is something that needs to be cleared. We could have, let's say, a **self-attending device**. That's a different story." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Multiple_Clearinghouse_Selection-August-27-2026.md]]
- **The field use-case Andrei brought back from the day before (not what Val was asking for).** Some locations run more than one card provider at once — the transcript's example is "**FirePay** plus income" (rendered "**Fiery Pay**" a few lines later; JVL's known competitor of that name is on the [[wiki/meetings/2026-08-12-fiery-pay-competitive-analysis.md|Aug-12 competitive analysis]]). If a player wins **$8,000** and one supplier's daily cap — "let's say **5K**" — is reached through InComm, "the location still has an opportunity to issue the rest of **3K**" through the other. This is the same two-provider pattern Jason Hall described on Aug 12 (credit limits pushing locations to sign a second provider), now seen from the device side. Val's answer: "**The goal is to have that choice, but I'm not necessarily saying that it should be the location that chooses it.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Multiple_Clearinghouse_Selection-August-27-2026.md]]
- **A separate 4:00 PM session the same day** was called on "the additional features that you are proposing for **printer and the dispenser**" (Val to Andrei; Andrei proposed 4 PM, Val agreed and said he had sent the invite). No content from that session is in this batch. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Multiple_Clearinghouse_Selection-August-27-2026.md]]
- **§15.8:** clear — clearinghouse/daily-cap economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Open questions

- Flex-Recycler **server topology** — an in-machine server (auto-expiring tokens, 24h reset) is sufficient per Val, but JVL is also building an external server-side component for operator reporting/analytics and recurring revenue; where one player's e-wallet transaction state lives across machines is unresolved (no formal ADR). — [[raw/Meeting_Notes_flex_recycler_req_daily_sync-April-29-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_flex_recycler_req_daily_sync-April-29-2026.md]]
- Flex-Recycler pricing model — **lease-with-deposit** (matching Vanilla/Pace) vs outright hardware sale; not decided. — [[raw/Meeting_Notes_flex_recycler_req_daily_sync-April-29-2026.md]]
- Where does the redemption device belong — behind the cage or self-service on the floor? Val: "it's to be decided"; he doubts fully self-serve ("If this were possible, then Amazon self-checkout stores would prosper everywhere"). — [[raw/flex_pay_evolution-april-8-2025.md]]
- Wallet validity window: 30 days vs ~24 hours — needs master feedback. — [[raw/flex_port_recap_felipe_juan_pavel-april-8-2025.md]]
- Can JVL's cage software run on a location's existing POS hardware? Complicated by mixed OSes and the player-PIN entry need — left open. — [[raw/evolution_flex_pay-april-11-2025.md]]
- How is a Prize Card's value communicated to a machine with a **single SAS port** vs one with **two SAS ports** (raised on the Aug-6 Prize-Card discovery)? — [[raw/Flex-Port-use_case-August-6-2025.md]]
- How does **PAX handle cash-outs above the per-card limit** ($1,000)? Alan and team have not answered; JVL's "don't process above-limit at all" rule stays provisional until today's PAX cycle is understood. (Raised Jul-22.) — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MG_IQ_Escrow_Transaction_Flow_Complete-July-22-2026.md]]
- How to detect that an **IQ changed location** post-MVP — GPS won't work indoors and the external IP address is unreliable. (Raised by Pavel, Jul-21/Jul-22; Val's Jul-30 proposal is to "quietly" listen to **LTE-modem triangulation + the Mutha Goose serial number** rather than actively police it.) — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MG_IQ_Escrow_Transaction_Flow_Complete-July-22-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]]
- Should JVL move to **operator-driven unit-to-location binding** (vs today's manual binding in the payment hub), and if so what binding procedure — semi- or fully-automatic? Direction leans operator-driven but is not finalized; the whole verification problem was parked for the pilot. (Raised by Andrei, Jul-30.) — [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]]
- Should JVL **register credit/units against a company's title** (as done conceptually with loans) to protect collections when locations or companies are sold — weighed against the operator friction it creates? (Raised by Val, Jul-30; unresolved.) — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]]
- How is **Fiery Pay** legally converting a non-cash MasterCard gift card into player points/cash, and who holds the merchant ID / reseller status — Fiery Pay or the location? (Raised by Juan and Val, Aug-12; unresolved.) — [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Fiery_Pay_Competitive_Analysis-August-12-2026.md]]
- Does JVL's recycler answer need **IQ**, or is recycler + flex port enough? Val leaned toward not needing IQ but expects operators will still take it — left open. And what is actually inside the "JVL kit for flex" box an operator is installing with a competitor system? (Aug-12.) — [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Fiery_Pay_Competitive_Analysis-August-12-2026.md]]

*(Resolved Jul-22: "Does MuthaGoose support an escrow (hold-then-validate) mode?" — answered **no** (Pavel); JVL uses the print-ticket-then-validate-in-parallel flow. See the Jul-22 section above.)*
